use core::{cell::UnsafeCell, hint::spin_loop, ops::{Deref, DerefMut}, sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering}};

pub struct SpinLock<T> { held: AtomicBool, value: UnsafeCell<T> }
unsafe impl<T: Send> Sync for SpinLock<T> {}
unsafe impl<T: Send> Send for SpinLock<T> {}
impl<T> SpinLock<T> {
    pub const fn new(value: T) -> Self { Self { held: AtomicBool::new(false), value: UnsafeCell::new(value) } }
    pub fn lock(&self) -> Guard<'_, T> {
        while self.held.compare_exchange_weak(false, true, Ordering::Acquire, Ordering::Relaxed).is_err() {
            while self.held.load(Ordering::Relaxed) { spin_loop(); }
        }
        Guard { lock: self }
    }
}
pub struct Guard<'a, T> { lock: &'a SpinLock<T> }
impl<T> Deref for Guard<'_, T> { type Target=T; fn deref(&self)->&T { unsafe { &*self.lock.value.get() } } }
impl<T> DerefMut for Guard<'_, T> { fn deref_mut(&mut self)->&mut T { unsafe { &mut *self.lock.value.get() } } }
impl<T> Drop for Guard<'_, T> { fn drop(&mut self) { self.lock.held.store(false, Ordering::Release); } }

/// Spin lock that cannot be interrupted on the local CPU while held.
pub struct IrqSpinLock<T>{inner:SpinLock<T>}
impl<T> IrqSpinLock<T>{pub const fn new(value:T)->Self{Self{inner:SpinLock::new(value)}}pub fn lock(&self)->IrqGuard<'_,T>{let flags=irq_save();IrqGuard{guard:core::mem::ManuallyDrop::new(self.inner.lock()),flags}}}
pub struct IrqGuard<'a,T>{guard:core::mem::ManuallyDrop<Guard<'a,T>>,flags:u64}
impl<T>Deref for IrqGuard<'_,T>{type Target=T;fn deref(&self)->&T{&self.guard}}
impl<T>DerefMut for IrqGuard<'_,T>{fn deref_mut(&mut self)->&mut T{&mut self.guard}}
impl<T>Drop for IrqGuard<'_,T>{fn drop(&mut self){unsafe{core::mem::ManuallyDrop::drop(&mut self.guard)}irq_restore(self.flags)}}
#[inline]fn irq_save()->u64{let flags:u64;unsafe{core::arch::asm!("pushfq","pop {}","cli",out(reg)flags,options(nomem));}flags}
#[inline]fn irq_restore(flags:u64){if flags&(1<<9)!=0{unsafe{core::arch::asm!("sti",options(nomem,nostack,preserves_flags));}}}

//! Single-CPU round-robin scheduler, task lifecycle and process ownership.
use core::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool,AtomicU32,Ordering};
use crate::process::Process;
use crate::sync::IrqSpinLock;
const MAX_TASKS:usize=8;const STACK_SIZE:usize=32768;
#[repr(align(16))]struct Stack([u8;STACK_SIZE]);
#[derive(Clone,Copy,PartialEq)]pub enum State{Unused,Running,Runnable,Sleeping,Stopped,Zombie}
#[derive(Clone,Copy)]pub struct Task{pub pid:u32,pub ppid:u32,pub pgid:u32,pub state:State,pub rsp:u64,pub kernel_stack_top:u64,pub cr3:u64,pub fs_base:u64,pub wake_tick:u64,pub wake_pending:bool,pub exit_code:i32,pub term_signal:u8,pub user_rsp:u64,pub syscall_frame:u64}
const EMPTY:Task=Task{pid:0,ppid:0,pgid:0,state:State::Unused,rsp:0,kernel_stack_top:0,cr3:0,fs_base:0,wake_tick:0,wake_pending:false,exit_code:0,term_signal:0,user_rsp:0,syscall_frame:0};
struct Scheduler{tasks:[Task;MAX_TASKS],current:usize,next_pid:u32}
static SCHED:IrqSpinLock<Scheduler>=IrqSpinLock::new(Scheduler{tasks:[EMPTY;MAX_TASKS],current:0,next_pid:2});
/* One Process per user task slot; kernel-only tasks leave their entry empty. */
static PROCS:IrqSpinLock<[Option<Process>;MAX_TASKS]>=IrqSpinLock::new([const{None};MAX_TASKS]);

pub fn current_pid()->u32{PID.load(Ordering::Relaxed)}
pub fn current_ppid()->u32{let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks[s.current].ppid}
pub fn current_pgid()->u32{let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks[s.current].pgid}

/* Locks are never nested the other way around: the scheduler guard is released
   before PROCS is taken, so closure bodies may lock anything but SCHED. */
pub fn with_current_process<R>(f:impl FnOnce(&mut Process)->R)->Option<R>{let slot=SCHED.lock().current;let mut procs=PROCS.lock();procs[slot].as_mut().map(f)}
pub fn has_current_process()->bool{let slot=SCHED.lock().current;PROCS.lock()[slot].is_some()}
/* Looks a process up by pid so that a signal can be queued on it. */
pub fn with_process_by_pid<R>(pid:u32,f:impl FnOnce(&mut Process)->R)->Option<R>{
 let slot={let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks.iter().position(|t|t.pid==pid&&t.state!=State::Unused&&t.state!=State::Zombie)}?;
 let mut procs=PROCS.lock();procs[slot].as_mut().map(f)
}
/* The processes a kill(pid, sig) request selects, following the Linux rules for
   a pid, a process group and the "every process" form. */
pub struct Targets{pub count:usize,pub pids:[u32;MAX_TASKS]}
pub fn signal_targets(pid:i64)->Targets{
 let me=current_pid();let my_pgid=current_pgid();let mut out=Targets{count:0,pids:[0;MAX_TASKS]};
 let s=SCHED.lock();
 for t in s.tasks.iter(){
  if t.state==State::Unused||t.pid==0{continue}
  let hit=if pid>0{t.pid==pid as u32}else if pid==0{t.pgid==my_pgid}else if pid==-1{t.pid!=me}else{t.pgid==(-pid) as u32};
  if hit&&out.count<MAX_TASKS{out.pids[out.count]=t.pid;out.count+=1;}
 }
 out
}
fn take_slot(s:&mut Scheduler)->Result<usize,i64>{(1..MAX_TASKS).find(|&i|s.tasks[i].state==State::Unused).ok_or(crate::errno::EAGAIN)}
fn alloc_pid(s:&mut Scheduler)->u32{let pid=s.next_pid;s.next_pid=s.next_pid.wrapping_add(1).max(2);pid}
fn build_kernel_stack(slot:usize,entry:u64)->u64{let top=unsafe{core::ptr::addr_of!(STACKS[slot].0)as u64+STACK_SIZE as u64};let mut sp=(top&!15)-8;unsafe{sp-=8;*(sp as*mut u64)=entry;for _ in 0..6{sp-=8;*(sp as*mut u64)=0;}}sp}
pub fn spawn_kernel(entry:fn())->Result<u32,i64>{let mut s=SCHED.lock();let slot=take_slot(&mut s)?;let pid=alloc_pid(&mut s);let top=unsafe{core::ptr::addr_of!(STACKS[slot].0)as u64+STACK_SIZE as u64};let sp=build_kernel_stack(slot,kernel_thread_trampoline as *const () as u64);KERNEL_ENTRIES.lock()[slot]=Some(entry);let cr3=crate::arch::x86_64::paging::PageTableRoot::current().pml4;let ppid=s.tasks[s.current].pid;let pgid=s.tasks[s.current].pgid;s.tasks[slot]=Task{pid,ppid,pgid,state:State::Runnable,rsp:sp,kernel_stack_top:top,cr3,fs_base:0,wake_tick:0,wake_pending:false,exit_code:0,term_signal:0,user_rsp:0,syscall_frame:0};Ok(pid)}

pub fn spawn_fork(mut child:Process,frame:crate::arch::x86_64::syscall::UserFrame)->Result<u32,i64>{let child_cr3=child.cr3;let mut s=SCHED.lock();let slot=match take_slot(&mut s){Ok(v)=>v,Err(e)=>{drop(s);child.abandon();return Err(e)}};let pid=alloc_pid(&mut s);let parent=s.current;let ppid=s.tasks[parent].pid;let pgid=s.tasks[parent].pgid;let fs_base=s.tasks[parent].fs_base;let top=unsafe{core::ptr::addr_of!(STACKS[slot].0)as u64+STACK_SIZE as u64};let sp=build_kernel_stack(slot,fork_thread_trampoline as *const () as u64);FORK_RESUMES.lock()[slot]=Some(frame);PROCS.lock()[slot]=Some(child);s.tasks[slot]=Task{pid,ppid,pgid,state:State::Runnable,rsp:sp,kernel_stack_top:top,cr3:child_cr3,fs_base,wake_tick:0,wake_pending:false,exit_code:0,term_signal:0,user_rsp:0,syscall_frame:0};Ok(pid)}
pub fn fork_process(frame:crate::arch::x86_64::syscall::UserFrame)->Result<u32,i64>{let child_cr3=match with_current_process(|p|p.clone_address_space()){Some(Ok(v))=>v,Some(Err(e))=>return Err(e),None=>return Err(crate::errno::ESRCH)};let child=match with_current_process(|p|p.fork_child(child_cr3)){Some(Ok(v))=>v,Some(Err(e))=>return Err(e),None=>return Err(crate::errno::ESRCH)};spawn_fork(child,frame)}
extern "sysv64" fn user_thread_trampoline()->!{let slot=SCHED.lock().current;let loaded=USER_STARTS.lock()[slot].take().unwrap();crate::println!("task: entering user pid={} rip={:#x} rsp={:#x}",current_pid(),loaded.entry,loaded.rsp);unsafe{crate::arch::x86_64::user::enter(loaded.entry,loaded.rsp)}}

pub fn yield_now(){schedule(false)}
pub fn on_timer_tick(){NEED_RESCHED.store(true,Ordering::Release);wake_sleepers(crate::arch::x86_64::apic::ticks());}
pub fn take_need_resched()->bool{NEED_RESCHED.swap(false,Ordering::AcqRel)}
/* A wake that lands before the target blocks is latched in wake_pending, so a
   register-then-block waiter can never lose it. */
pub fn sleep_until(tick:u64){let mut s=SCHED.lock();let current=s.current;if s.tasks[current].wake_pending{s.tasks[current].wake_pending=false;return}s.tasks[current].wake_tick=tick;s.tasks[current].state=State::Sleeping;drop(s);schedule(true)}
fn wake_sleepers(now:u64){let mut s=SCHED.lock();for t in &mut s.tasks{if t.state==State::Sleeping&&t.wake_pending{t.wake_pending=false;t.state=State::Runnable}else if t.state==State::Sleeping&&t.wake_tick<=now{t.state=State::Runnable;}}}
pub fn block_current(){let mut s=SCHED.lock();let current=s.current;if s.tasks[current].wake_pending{s.tasks[current].wake_pending=false;return}s.tasks[current].state=State::Sleeping;s.tasks[current].wake_tick=u64::MAX;drop(s);schedule(true)}
/* Suspends the current task until a SIGCONT makes it runnable again. */
pub fn stop_current(){
 {let mut s=SCHED.lock();let current=s.current;s.tasks[current].state=State::Stopped;s.tasks[current].wake_pending=false;}
 loop{
  if schedule_stopped(){let mut s=SCHED.lock();let c=s.current;s.tasks[c].state=State::Running;return}
  let resumed={let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks[s.current].state!=State::Stopped};
  if resumed{let mut s=SCHED.lock();let c=s.current;s.tasks[c].state=State::Running;return}
  unsafe{core::arch::asm!("sti","hlt","cli",options(nomem,nostack));}
 }
}
/* Resumes a stopped task. Returns false when the task was not stopped. */
pub fn continue_pid(pid:u32)->bool{
 let mut s=SCHED.lock();
 if let Some(t)=s.tasks.iter_mut().find(|t|t.pid==pid&&t.state!=State::Unused&&t.state!=State::Zombie){
  if t.state==State::Stopped{t.state=State::Runnable;t.wake_pending=false;true}else{false}
 }else{false}
}
/* Schedules another task while the current one is stopped. Returns false when
   no other task can run. */
fn schedule_stopped()->bool{
 let(old_ptr,next)= {
  let mut s=SCHED.lock();let old=s.current;save_syscall_context(&mut s);let mut next=old;
  for step in 1..=MAX_TASKS{let i=(old+step)%MAX_TASKS;if s.tasks[i].state==State::Runnable{next=i;break}}
  if next==old{return false}
  s.tasks[next].state=State::Running;s.current=next;(&mut s.tasks[old].rsp as*mut u64,s.tasks[next])};
 PID.store(next.pid,Ordering::Relaxed);
 unsafe{apply_arch_state(next);switch_context(old_ptr,next.rsp)}
 restore_syscall_context();
 true
}
/* The syscall entry state belongs to the task that owns it. A task that blocks
   inside a syscall must find its own user stack pointer and register frame
   when the scheduler brings it back. */
fn save_syscall_context(s:&mut Scheduler){
 let c=s.current;
 unsafe{s.tasks[c].user_rsp=crate::arch::x86_64::syscall::SYSCALL_USER_RSP;s.tasks[c].syscall_frame=crate::arch::x86_64::syscall::SYSCALL_FRAME;}
}
fn restore_syscall_context(){
 let s=SCHED.lock();let c=s.current;
 unsafe{crate::arch::x86_64::syscall::SYSCALL_USER_RSP=s.tasks[c].user_rsp;crate::arch::x86_64::syscall::SYSCALL_FRAME=s.tasks[c].syscall_frame;}
}
pub fn wake_pid(pid:u32)->bool{let mut s=SCHED.lock();if let Some(t)=s.tasks.iter_mut().find(|t|t.pid==pid&&t.state!=State::Unused&&t.state!=State::Zombie){if t.state==State::Sleeping{t.state=State::Runnable}else{t.wake_pending=true}true}else{false}}
fn finish(code:i32,signal:u8)->!{{let mut s=SCHED.lock();let current=s.current;s.tasks[current].state=State::Zombie;s.tasks[current].exit_code=code;s.tasks[current].term_signal=signal;let parent=s.tasks[current].ppid;drop(s);if parent!=0{let _=wake_pid(parent);}}loop{schedule(true);unsafe{core::arch::asm!("hlt",options(nomem,nostack));}}}
pub fn finish_current(code:i32)->!{finish(code,0)}
pub fn exit(code:i32)->!{crate::println!("task {} exited {}",current_pid(),code);with_current_process(|proc|{crate::println!("task exited status={}",code);proc.teardown_exit(code,0)});finish(code,0)}

   does. The parent observes the death through the wait status. */

pub enum WaitOutcome{Reaped(u32,i32),NoHang,Blocked}

pub fn reap_child(pid_sel:i64,nohang:bool)->Result<WaitOutcome,i64>{
 let me=current_pid();let my_pgid={let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks[s.current].pgid};
 let matches=|t:&Task|t.ppid==me&&(pid_sel==-1||(pid_sel==0&&t.pgid==my_pgid)||(pid_sel>0&&t.pid==pid_sel as u32)||(pid_sel< -1&&t.pgid==(-pid_sel) as u32));

//! Monotonic bootstrap time and xorshift64 random source.

const NS_PER_TICK:u64=1_000_000;
static RNG:AtomicU64=AtomicU64::new(0x6e6f_7274_6873_7461);
pub fn monotonic_ns()->u64{crate::arch::x86_64::apic::ticks().saturating_mul(NS_PER_TICK)}
pub fn ticks_after_ns(ns:u64)->u64{crate::arch::x86_64::apic::ticks().saturating_add(ns.saturating_add(NS_PER_TICK-1)/NS_PER_TICK)}

//! Allocation-free initramfs VFS with read/write support.
use crate::{errno,sync::SpinLock};
pub type Result<T>=core::result::Result<T,i64>;
pub const S_IFREG:u32=0o100000;pub const S_IFDIR:u32=0o040000;const MAX_NODES:usize=32;const NAME_MAX:usize=127;
#[derive(Clone,Copy)]pub struct Inode{pub ino:u64,pub mode:u32,pub size:u64,pub data:*mut u8,pub name:[u8;NAME_MAX+1],pub name_len:usize}
unsafe impl Send for Inode{} unsafe impl Sync for Inode{}
const EMPTY:Inode=Inode{ino:0,mode:0,size:0,data:core::ptr::null_mut(),name:[0;NAME_MAX+1],name_len:0};
struct Mount{nodes:[Inode;MAX_NODES],len:usize,mounted:bool}
static ROOT:SpinLock<Mount>=SpinLock::new(Mount{nodes:[EMPTY;MAX_NODES],len:0,mounted:false});
fn u16le(b:&[u8],o:usize)->Result<u16>{let x=b.get(o..o+2).ok_or(errno::EINVAL)?;Ok(u16::from_le_bytes([x[0],x[1]]))}
fn u32le(b:&[u8],o:usize)->Result<u32>{let x=b.get(o..o+4).ok_or(errno::EINVAL)?;Ok(u32::from_le_bytes([x[0],x[1],x[2],x[3]]))}
fn align8(v:usize)->Result<usize>{v.checked_add(7).map(|x|x&!7).ok_or(errno::EINVAL)}
pub fn mount_initramfs(image:&'static [u8])->Result<usize>{if image.get(..8)!=Some(b"NSAR0001"){return Err(errno::EINVAL)}let count=u32le(image,8)? as usize;if count>MAX_NODES{return Err(errno::ENOMEM)}let mut m=ROOT.lock();m.len=0;let mut p=12usize;for i in 0..count{let nl=u16le(image,p)? as usize;let mode=u16le(image,p+2)? as u32;let size=u32le(image,p+4)? as usize;p+=8;if nl==0||nl>NAME_MAX{return Err(errno::EINVAL)}let n=image.get(p..p+nl).ok_or(errno::EINVAL)?;if n[0]!=b'/'||n.contains(&0){return Err(errno::EINVAL)}let mut name=[0u8;NAME_MAX+1];name[..nl].copy_from_slice(n);p=align8(p+nl)?;let data=image.get(p..p+size).ok_or(errno::EINVAL)?;m.nodes[i]=Inode{ino:(i+2)as u64,mode:S_IFREG|mode as u32&0o777,size:size as u64,data:data.as_ptr()as*mut u8,name,name_len:nl};p=align8(p+size)?;}m.len=count;m.mounted=true;Ok(count)}
pub fn lookup(path:&str)->Result<Inode>{if !path.starts_with('/')||path.as_bytes().contains(&0){return Err(errno::ENOENT)}let m=ROOT.lock();if !m.mounted{return Err(errno::ENOENT)}m.nodes[..m.len].iter().copied().find(|n|&n.name[..n.name_len]==path.as_bytes()).ok_or(errno::ENOENT)}
pub fn read(inode:Inode,off:u64,dst:&mut[u8])->Result<usize>{if off>=inode.size{return Ok(0)}let n=core::cmp::min(dst.len(),(inode.size-off)as usize);let src=unsafe{core::slice::from_raw_parts(inode.data.add(off as usize),n)};dst[..n].copy_from_slice(src);Ok(n)}
pub fn write(inode:Inode,off:u64,src:&[u8])->Result<usize>{
 let m=ROOT.lock();let idx=m.nodes[..m.len].iter().position(|&n|n.ino==inode.ino).ok_or(errno::ENOENT)?;
 let end=off.checked_add(src.len() as u64).ok_or(errno::EINVAL)?;

 if end>capacity{return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
 unsafe{core::slice::from_raw_parts_mut(m.nodes[idx].data.add(off as usize),src.len()).copy_from_slice(src)}

}
pub fn create(path:&str,mode:u32)->Result<Inode>{
 let mut m=ROOT.lock();
 if !path.starts_with('/')||path.as_bytes().contains(&0){return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
 if m.len>=MAX_NODES{return Err(errno::EMFILE)}
 if m.nodes[..m.len].iter().any(|n|&n.name[..n.name_len]==path.as_bytes()){return Err(errno::EEXIST)}
 let name_len=path.len();
 if name_len>NAME_MAX{return Err(errno::ENAMETOOLONG)}
  let ino=(m.len+2)as u64;
  let mut name=[0u8;NAME_MAX+1];name[..name_len].copy_from_slice(path.as_bytes());
  let node=Inode{ino,mode:S_IFREG|mode&0o777,size:0,data:core::ptr::null_mut(),name,name_len};
  let idx=m.len; m.nodes[idx]=node;
  m.len+=1;
  Ok(node)
}
pub fn unlink(path:&str)->Result<()>{
 let mut m=ROOT.lock();
 let idx=m.nodes[..m.len].iter().position(|n|&n.name[..n.name_len]==path.as_bytes()).ok_or(errno::ENOENT)?;
 if m.nodes[idx].mode&S_IFDIR!=0{return Err(errno::EISDIR)}
 for i in idx..m.len-1{m.nodes[i]=m.nodes[i+1];}
 m.len-=1;
 Ok(())
}
pub fn mkdir(path:&str,mode:u32)->Result<Inode>{

}
pub fn readdir(path:&str)->Result<usize>{
 let m=ROOT.lock();

}

//! Architecture-neutral virtual-memory area ledger. Hardware PTE work stays in arch.
pub const PAGE_SIZE:usize=4096; pub const MAX_AREAS:usize=64;
// Anonymous mappings live in PML4 slot 129: the 64 GiB firmware identity
// map in slot 0 and the ELF/stack slots stay untouched.
pub const MMAP_BASE:usize=0x0000_40c0_0000_0000;
#[derive(Clone,Copy,Default,PartialEq,Eq)] pub struct VmFlags(pub u8);
impl VmFlags { pub const READ:Self=Self(1);pub const WRITE:Self=Self(2);pub const EXEC:Self=Self(4);pub const USER:Self=Self(8);pub const ANON:Self=Self(16);pub fn contains(self,x:Self)->bool{self.0&x.0==x.0} }
#[derive(Clone,Copy,Default)] pub struct Area{pub start:usize,pub end:usize,pub flags:VmFlags}
#[derive(Clone)] pub struct Map{areas:[Area;MAX_AREAS],len:usize,next_hint:usize}
impl Map{
 pub const fn new()->Self{Self{areas:[Area{start:0,end:0,flags:VmFlags(0)};MAX_AREAS],len:0,next_hint:MMAP_BASE}}
 pub fn insert(&mut self,start:usize,len:usize,flags:VmFlags)->Result<(),i64>{
  if start%PAGE_SIZE!=0||len==0{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)} let end=start.checked_add(align_up(len)?).ok_or(crate::errno::ENOMEM)?;
  if start<crate::usercopy::USER_MIN||end>crate::usercopy::USER_MAX{return Err(crate::errno::ENOMEM)}
  if self.len==MAX_AREAS{return Err(crate::errno::ENOMEM)}
  if self.areas[..self.len].iter().any(|a|start<a.end&&a.start<end){return Err(crate::errno::EEXIST)}
  let mut at=self.len;for i in 0..self.len{if start<self.areas[i].start{at=i;break}}
  for i in (at..self.len).rev(){self.areas[i+1]=self.areas[i]} self.areas[at]=Area{start,end,flags};self.len+=1;Ok(())
 }
 pub fn allocate(&mut self,len:usize,flags:VmFlags)->Result<usize,i64>{let size=align_up(len)?;let mut p=self.next_hint;loop{let candidate_end=p.checked_add(size).ok_or(crate::errno::ENOMEM)?;let mut collision=None;for a in &self.areas[..self.len]{if p<a.end&&a.start<candidate_end{collision=Some(a.end);break}}match collision{Some(n)=>{p=align_up(n)?;if p.checked_add(size).filter(|e|*e<=crate::usercopy::USER_MAX).is_none(){return Err(crate::errno::ENOMEM)}},None=>break}}self.insert(p,size,flags)?;self.next_hint=p+size;Ok(p)}
 pub fn remove(&mut self,start:usize,len:usize)->Result<(),i64>{let end=start.checked_add(align_up(len)?).ok_or(crate::errno::EINVAL)?;let i=self.areas[..self.len].iter().position(|a|a.start==start&&a.end==end).ok_or(crate::errno::EINVAL)?;for j in i+1..self.len{self.areas[j-1]=self.areas[j]}self.len-=1;Ok(())}
 pub fn protect(&mut self,start:usize,len:usize,flags:VmFlags)->Result<(),i64>{let end=start.checked_add(align_up(len)?).ok_or(crate::errno::EINVAL)?;let a=self.areas[..self.len].iter_mut().find(|a|a.start==start&&a.end==end).ok_or(crate::errno::EINVAL)?;a.flags=flags;Ok(())}
 pub fn validate(&self,ptr:usize,len:usize,write:bool)->bool{let end=match ptr.checked_add(len){Some(v)=>v,None=>return false};self.areas[..self.len].iter().any(|a|ptr>=a.start&&end<=a.end&&a.flags.contains(VmFlags::USER)&&(!write||a.flags.contains(VmFlags::WRITE)))}
}
fn align_up(v:usize)->Result<usize,i64>{if v==0{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}v.checked_add(PAGE_SIZE-1).map(|x|x&!(PAGE_SIZE-1)).ok_or(crate::errno::ENOMEM)}

//! Fixed-capacity wait queues backed by scheduler task slots.
use crate::sync::IrqSpinLock;
const MAX_WAITERS:usize=8;
#[derive(Clone,Copy)]struct Waiter{pid:u32,used:bool}
const EMPTY:Waiter=Waiter{pid:0,used:false};
pub struct WaitQueue{waiters:IrqSpinLock<[Waiter;MAX_WAITERS]>}
impl WaitQueue{
 pub const fn new()->Self{Self{waiters:IrqSpinLock::new([EMPTY;MAX_WAITERS])}}
 /** Enlists the current task without blocking. Callers that observe the wait
     condition under their own lock use this, then drop it and call wait(). */
 pub fn register(&self)->Result<(),i64>{let pid=crate::task::current_pid();let mut q=self.waiters.lock();let slot=q.iter_mut().find(|w|!w.used).ok_or(crate::errno::EAGAIN)?;*slot=Waiter{pid,used:true};Ok(())}
 pub fn wait(&self){crate::task::block_current()}
 pub fn sleep(&self)->Result<(),i64>{self.register()?;self.wait();Ok(())}
 pub fn wake_one(&self)->bool{let pid={let mut q=self.waiters.lock();match q.iter_mut().find(|w|w.used){Some(w)=>{w.used=false;w.pid},None=>return false}};crate::task::wake_pid(pid)}
 pub fn wake_all(&self)->usize{let mut n=0;while self.wake_one(){n+=1}n}
}

#include <signal.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include "slop_internal.h"

#define SYS_KILL 62
#define SYS_RT_SIGACTION 13
#define SYS_RT_SIGPROCMASK 14
#define SYS_RT_SIGRETURN 15
#define SYS_RT_SIGPENDING 127

/* The kernel action layout: handler, flags, mask and restorer. The user
   visible struct sigaction keeps the POSIX order, so the wrapper translates. */
struct k_sigaction {
    void (*k_handler)(int);
    unsigned long k_flags;
    sigset_t k_mask;
    void (*k_restorer)(void);
};

/* The handler returns into this address, which performs the rt_sigreturn
   syscall and lets the kernel restore the interrupted context. */
extern void __slop_sigreturn_trampoline(void);

int kill(pid_t pid, int sig) {
    long r = __slop_syscall6(SYS_KILL, (long)pid, (long)sig, 0, 0, 0, 0);
    if (r < 0) {
        errno = (int)-r;
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

int raise(int sig) {
    return kill(getpid(), sig);
}

int sigaction(int sig, const struct sigaction *act, struct sigaction *oldact) {
    struct k_sigaction ka = {0, 0, 0, 0};
    struct k_sigaction ko = {0, 0, 0, 0};
    long r;
    if (act) {
        ka.k_handler = act->sa_handler;
        ka.k_flags = ((unsigned long)act->sa_flags & ~(unsigned long)SA_RESTORER) | SA_RESTORER;
        ka.k_mask = act->sa_mask;
        ka.k_restorer = act->sa_restorer ? act->sa_restorer : __slop_sigreturn_trampoline;
    }
    r = __slop_syscall6(SYS_RT_SIGACTION, (long)sig, act ? (long)&ka : 0,
                         oldact ? (long)&ko : 0, 0, 0, 0);
    if (r < 0) {
        errno = (int)-r;
        return -1;
    }
    if (oldact) {
        oldact->sa_handler = ko.k_handler;
        oldact->sa_mask = ko.k_mask;
        oldact->sa_flags = (int)ko.k_flags;
        oldact->sa_restorer = ko.k_restorer;
    }
    return 0;
}

int sigprocmask(int how, const sigset_t *set, sigset_t *oldset) {
    long r = __slop_syscall6(SYS_RT_SIGPROCMASK, (long)how, (long)set, (long)oldset, 0, 0, 0);
    if (r < 0) {
        errno = (int)-r;
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

int sigpending(sigset_t *set) {
    long r = __slop_syscall6(SYS_RT_SIGPENDING, (long)set, 8, 0, 0, 0, 0);
    if (r < 0) {
        errno = (int)-r;
        return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}

int sigemptyset(sigset_t *set) {
    *set = 0;
    return 0;
}

int sigaddset(sigset_t *set, int sig) {
    if (sig < 1 || sig > 63) return -1;
    *set |= 1UL << (sig - 1);
    return 0;
}

int sigdelset(sigset_t *set, int sig) {
    if (sig < 1 || sig > 63) return -1;
    *set &= ~(1UL << (sig - 1));
    return 0;
}

int sigismember(const sigset_t *set, int sig) {
    if (sig < 1 || sig > 63) return -1;
    return (int)((*set >> (sig - 1)) & 1UL);
}

void sigreturn(void) {
    __slop_syscall6(SYS_RT_SIGRETURN, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);
}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: libc/signal.c

/* The signal handler restorer. The kernel stores this address at the bottom of
   the signal frame, so a handler that returns with `ret` lands here and the
   rt_sigreturn syscall restores the interrupted context. */
.global __slop_sigreturn_trampoline
.type __slop_sigreturn_trampoline,@function
__slop_sigreturn_trampoline:
    mov $15, %eax              /* rt_sigreturn */
    syscall
    /* The kernel restores the interrupted context, so the syscall never
       returns here. */
1:  jmp 1b
.size __slop_sigreturn_trampoline,.-__slop_sigreturn_trampoline

/* Freestanding C startup. The kernel stack builder places argc at (%rsp),
   argv right after it and envp after the argv NULL terminator. */
.global _start
.type _start,@function
_start:
    xor %rbp, %rbp
    mov (%rsp), %rdi
    lea 8(%rsp), %rsi
    lea 16(%rsp,%rdi,8), %rdx
    mov %rdx, environ(%rip)
    and $-16, %rsp
    call main
    mov %eax, %edi
    mov $231, %eax
    syscall
1:  jmp 1b
.size _start, .-_start

================================================================================
FILE: libc/stdio.c
SIZE: 2403 bytes
SHA-256: 419b3f9fbfc93f03886ad5b2a3ae043147b5fc1553caa7bb8228ecec54290f64
================================================================================
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>

struct outbuf {
    char data[512];
    size_t len;
};

static void emit(struct outbuf *o, char c) {
    if (o->len < sizeof(o->data)) o->data[o->len++] = c;
}

static void emit_str(struct outbuf *o, const char *s) {
    while (*s) emit(o, *s++);
}

static void emit_uint(struct outbuf *o, unsigned long value, unsigned base, int upper) {
    char tmp[32];
    const char *digits = upper ? "0123456789ABCDEF" : "0123456789abcdef";
    int n = 0;
    if (value == 0) tmp[n++] = '0';
    while (value) {
        tmp[n++] = digits[value % base];
        value /= base;
    }
    while (n) emit(o, tmp[--n]);
}

static void format(struct outbuf *o, const char *fmt, va_list ap) {
    for (; *fmt; fmt++) {
        if (*fmt != '%') {
            emit(o, *fmt);
            continue;
        }
        switch (*++fmt) {
        case '%':
            emit(o, '%');
            break;
        case 'c':
            emit(o, (char)va_arg(ap, int));
            break;
        case 's': {
            const char *s = va_arg(ap, const char *);
            emit_str(o, s ? s : "(null)");
            break;
        }
        case 'd': {
            int v = va_arg(ap, int);
            if (v < 0) {
                emit(o, '-');
                emit_uint(o, (unsigned long)(-(long)v), 10, 0);
            } else {
                emit_uint(o, (unsigned long)v, 10, 0);
            }
            break;
        }
        case 'u':
            emit_uint(o, va_arg(ap, unsigned int), 10, 0);
            break;
        case 'x':
            emit_uint(o, va_arg(ap, unsigned int), 16, 0);
            break;
        case 'p':
            emit_str(o, "0x");
            emit_uint(o, (unsigned long)(size_t)va_arg(ap, void *), 16, 0);
            break;
        default:
            emit(o, '%');
            emit(o, *fmt);
            break;
        }
    }
}

int printf(const char *fmt, ...) {
    struct outbuf o;
    va_list ap;
    o.len = 0;
    va_start(ap, fmt);
    format(&o, fmt, ap);
    va_end(ap);
    if (write(1, o.data, o.len) != (ssize_t)o.len) return -1;
    return (int)o.len;
}

int puts(const char *s) {
    size_t n = strlen(s);
    if (write(1, s, n) != (ssize_t)n) return -1;
    if (write(1, "\n", 1) != 1) return -1;
    return 0;
}

int putchar(int c) {
    char b = (char)c;
    if (write(1, &b, 1) != 1) return -1;
    return c;
}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: libc/stdio.c

#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include "slop_internal.h"

/* First-fit free-list allocator over anonymous mappings. Block headers live
   inside the mapped chunk so free() can coalesce neighbours. */
struct block {
    size_t size;
    struct block *next;
    int free;
    size_t pad;
};

static struct block *arena;
char **environ;

static struct block *grow(size_t need) {
    size_t want = (need + sizeof(struct block) + 4095UL) & ~4095UL;
    void *mem = mmap(0, want, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE, MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS, -1, 0);
    if (mem == (void *)-1) return 0;
    struct block *b = mem;
    b->size = want - sizeof(struct block);
    b->free = 1;
    b->next = arena;
    arena = b;
    return b;
}

void *malloc(size_t n) {
    struct block *b;
    if (n == 0) n = 1;
    n = (n + 15UL) & ~15UL;
    for (b = arena; b; b = b->next) {
        if (!b->free || b->size < n) continue;
        if (b->size >= n + sizeof(struct block) + 16) {
            struct block *split = (struct block *)((char *)(b + 1) + n);
            split->size = b->size - n - sizeof(struct block);
            split->next = b->next;
            split->free = 1;
            split->pad = 0;
            b->next = split;
            b->size = n;
        }
        b->free = 0;
        return b + 1;
    }
    b = grow(n);
    if (!b) return 0;
    b->free = 0;
    return b + 1;
}

void free(void *p) {
    struct block *b;
    if (!p) return;
    b = (struct block *)p - 1;
    b->free = 1;
    for (b = arena; b && b->next; b = b->next) {
        if (b->free && b->next->free &&
            (char *)b + sizeof(struct block) + b->size == (char *)b->next) {
            b->size += sizeof(struct block) + b->next->size;
            b->next = b->next->next;
        }
    }
}

void *calloc(size_t count, size_t size) {
    size_t total;
    void *p;
    if (count && size > (size_t)-1 / count) return 0;
    total = count * size;
    p = malloc(total);
    if (p) memset(p, 0, total);
    return p;
}

void *realloc(void *p, size_t n) {
    struct block *b;
    void *q;
    if (!p) return malloc(n);
    if (n == 0) {
        free(p);
        return 0;
    }
    b = (struct block *)p - 1;
    if (b->size >= n) return p;
    q = malloc(n);
    if (!q) return 0;
    memcpy(q, p, b->size < n ? b->size : n);
    free(p);
    return q;
}

char *getenv(const char *name) {
    size_t len = strlen(name);
    char **e;
    if (!environ) return 0;
    for (e = environ; *e; e++) {
        if (strncmp(*e, name, len) == 0 && (*e)[len] == '=') return *e + len + 1;
    }
    return 0;
}

void exit(int status) {
    _exit(status);
}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: libc/stdlib.c

#include <string.h>

void *memcpy(void *dst, const void *src, size_t n) {
    unsigned char *d = dst;
    const unsigned char *s = src;
    while (n--) *d++ = *s++;
    return dst;
}

void *memmove(void *dst, const void *src, size_t n) {
    unsigned char *d = dst;
    const unsigned char *s = src;
    if (d < s) {
        while (n--) *d++ = *s++;
    } else {
        while (n--) d[n] = s[n];
    }
    return dst;
}

void *memset(void *dst, int c, size_t n) {
    unsigned char *d = dst;
    while (n--) *d++ = (unsigned char)c;
    return dst;
}

int memcmp(const void *a, const void *b, size_t n) {
    const unsigned char *x = a, *y = b;
    while (n--) {
        if (*x != *y) return (int)*x - (int)*y;
        x++;
        y++;
    }
    return 0;
}

size_t strlen(const char *s) {
    const char *p = s;
    while (*p) p++;
    return (size_t)(p - s);
}

int strcmp(const char *a, const char *b) {
    while (*a && *a == *b) {
        a++;
        b++;
    }
    return (int)(unsigned char)*a - (int)(unsigned char)*b;
}

int strncmp(const char *a, const char *b, size_t n) {
    while (n && *a && *a == *b) {
        a++;
        b++;
        n--;
    }
    if (n == 0) return 0;
    return (int)(unsigned char)*a - (int)(unsigned char)*b;
}

char *strchr(const char *s, int c) {
    for (;; s++) {
        if (*s == (char)c) return (char *)s;
        if (!*s) return 0;
    }
}

char *strcpy(char *dst, const char *src) {
    char *d = dst;
    while ((*d++ = *src++)) {
    }
    return dst;
}

/* long __slop_syscall6(long n, long a1, long a2, long a3, long a4, long a5, long a6)
   Translates the System V C argument registers to the Linux x86-64 syscall ABI. */
.global __slop_syscall6
.type __slop_syscall6,@function
__slop_syscall6:
    mov %rdi, %rax
    mov %rsi, %rdi
    mov %rdx, %rsi
    mov %rcx, %rdx
    mov %r8, %r10
    mov %r9, %r8
    mov 8(%rsp), %r9
    syscall
    ret
.size __slop_syscall6, .-__slop_syscall6

/* POSIX wrapper convention: report -1 and stash the raw errno value. */
static long sys_fail(long r) {
    if (r < 0) {
        errno = (int)-r;
        return -1;
    }
    return r;
}

ssize_t write(int fd, const void *buf, size_t n) {
    return (ssize_t)__slop_syscall6(SYS_WRITE, fd, (long)buf, (long)n, 0, 0, 0);
}

}

    return (int)__slop_syscall6(SYS_CLOSE, fd, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);
}

    long r = __slop_syscall6(SYS_MMAP, (long)addr, (long)len, prot, flags, fd, offset);
    if (r < 0) return (void *)-1;
    return (void *)r;
}

.text
.global slop_mnu_kernel_entry
slop_mnu_kernel_entry:
    /* Microsoft x64 caller enters with RSP % 16 == 8. kmain_bootinfo is SysV64,
       so align RSP to 16 before CALL and pass BootInfo in RDI. */
    mov g_boot_info(%rip), %rdi
    sub $8, %rsp
    call kmain_bootinfo
1:  cli
    hlt
    jmp 1b

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <sys/mman.h>

static int failures;

#define CHECK(cond, what)                     \
    do {                                      \
        if (!(cond)) {                        \
            puts("c runtime: failed: " what); \
            failures++;                       \
        }                                     \
    } while (0)

int main(int argc, char **argv, char **envp) {
    char a[16];
    char b[16];
    char *p;
    char *q;
    char *z;
    char fibuf[64];
    ssize_t n;
    int fd;
    int i;
    int zeroed;
    int written;

    puts("c runtime: main entered");
    CHECK(argc == 2, "argc");
    CHECK(argv != 0 && argv[1] != 0 && strcmp(argv[1], "from-asm") == 0, "argv[1]");
    CHECK(getenv("SLOP_MNU_C") != 0 && strcmp(getenv("SLOP_MNU_C"), "1") == 0, "envp");
    CHECK(envp != 0 && envp[0] != 0, "envp pointer");

    memset(a, 'A', 8);
    a[8] = 0;
    CHECK(strlen(a) == 8, "strlen");
    memcpy(b, a, 9);
    CHECK(memcmp(a, b, 9) == 0, "memcmp");
    CHECK(strcmp("slop", "slop") == 0 && strcmp("slop", "mnu") != 0, "strcmp");
    CHECK(strchr("slop/mnu", '/') != 0 && strchr("slop/mnu", '?') == 0, "strchr");

    p = malloc(40);
    CHECK(p != 0, "malloc");
    if (p) memset(p, 0x5a, 40);
    q = malloc(40);
    CHECK(q != 0, "malloc second");
    free(q);
    q = malloc(40);
    CHECK(q != 0, "malloc reuse");
    zeroed = 1;
    z = calloc(4, 16);
    CHECK(z != 0, "calloc");
    if (z) {
        for (i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
            if (z[i]) zeroed = 0;
        }
    }
    CHECK(zeroed, "calloc zero");
    if (p) {
        for (i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
            if (((unsigned char *)p)[i] != 0x5a) {
                CHECK(0, "malloc content");
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    z = realloc(z, 128);
    CHECK(z != 0, "realloc");
    free(z);
    free(p);
    free(q);

    fd = openat(AT_FDCWD, "/etc/motd", O_RDONLY, 0);
    CHECK(fd >= 0, "openat");
    if (fd >= 0) {
        n = read(fd, fibuf, 63);
        CHECK(n > 0, "read");
        if (n > 0) {
            fibuf[n] = 0;
            CHECK(strcmp(fibuf, "Slop/MNU initramfs\n") == 0, "motd content");
        }
        CHECK(close(fd) == 0, "close");
    }

    written = printf("c runtime: printf %s %d %u %x\n", "works", -7, 42u, 0x2a);
    CHECK(written > 0, "printf");

    if (failures) {
        printf("c runtime: %d checks failed\n", failures);
        return 1;
    }
    puts("c runtime: passed");
    {
        char *av[] = {"hello-posix", "from-c", 0};
        char *ev[] = {"SLOP_MNU_POSIX=1", 0};
        execve("/bin/hello-posix", av, ev);
    }
    puts("c runtime: failed: execve /bin/hello-posix");
    return 1;
}

.section .text
_start:
    cmpq $1,(%rsp); jne failure
    mov 8(%rsp),%rax; cmpl $0x6c6c6568,(%rax); jne failure
    mov 24(%rsp),%rax; test %rax,%rax; je failure
    cmpl $0x504f4c53,(%rax); jne failure

    mov %rax,%rdx; mov $1,%rax; mov $1,%rdi; lea buffer(%rip),%rsi; syscall

FILE: tests/hello-posix.c
SIZE: 2286 bytes
SHA-256: 9fd7303e54f509a67ae3c3224b8c3196487d369b5a926e371a479144ab91333c
================================================================================
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <sys/wait.h>

static int failures;

#define CHECK(cond, what)                       \
    do {                                        \
        if (!(cond)) {                          \
            puts("posix: failed: " what);       \
            failures++;                         \
        }                                       \
    } while (0)

int main(int argc, char **argv, char **envp) {
    int fds[2];
    char buf[64];
    ssize_t n;
    pid_t pid;
    pid_t me;
    int status;
    int got;

    puts("posix: main entered");
    CHECK(argc == 2 && argv[1] != 0 && strcmp(argv[1], "from-c") == 0, "argv");
    CHECK(getenv("SLOP_MNU_POSIX") != 0 && strcmp(getenv("SLOP_MNU_POSIX"), "1") == 0, "envp");
    (void)envp;
    me = getpid();
    CHECK(me > 1, "getpid");
    CHECK(getppid() == 1, "getppid");
    CHECK(pipe(fds) == 0, "pipe");

    pid = fork();
    CHECK(pid >= 0, "fork");
    if (pid == 0) {
        /* Child: verify the new pid, then route stdout through the pipe. */
        CHECK(getpid() != me, "child pid");
        close(fds[0]);
        CHECK(dup2(fds[1], 1) == 1, "dup2");
        close(fds[1]);
        if (failures) _exit(8);
        puts("posix: child message 42");
        _exit(failures ? 8 : 7);
    }

    CHECK(pid > 0, "fork parent pid");
    close(fds[1]);
    got = 0;
    for (;;) {
        if (got >= (int)sizeof(buf) - 1) break;
        n = read(fds[0], buf + got, sizeof(buf) - 1 - got);
        if (n <= 0) break;
        got += (int)n;
    }
    buf[got > 0 ? got : 0] = 0;
    CHECK(got > 0, "pipe read");
    CHECK(strcmp(buf, "posix: child message 42\n") == 0, "pipe content");
    CHECK(close(fds[0]) == 0, "close read end");

    CHECK(waitpid(pid, &status, 0) == pid, "waitpid");
    CHECK(WIFEXITED(status) && WEXITSTATUS(status) == 7, "child status");

    if (failures) {
        printf("posix: %d checks failed\n", failures);
        return 1;
    }
    puts("posix: fork, pipe, dup2 and wait4 passed");
    {
        char *av[] = {"hello-signal", "from-posix", 0};
        char *ev[] = {"SLOP_MNU_SIGNAL=1", 0};
        execve("/bin/hello-signal", av, ev);
    }
    puts("posix: failed: execve /bin/hello-signal");
    return 1;
}

END FILE: tests/hello-posix.c

================================================================================
FILE: tests/hello-signal.c
SIZE: 5271 bytes
SHA-256: 53d296a86aedc0837e5f2182cedcde19282a60e08413facfee3edce9faa7ffb6
================================================================================
#include <signal.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <time.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <sys/wait.h>

static int failures;

#define CHECK(cond, what)                       \
    do {                                        \
        if (!(cond)) {                          \
            puts("signals: failed: " what);     \
            failures++;                         \
        }                                       \
    } while (0)

/* The handler records what the kernel delivered. The variables are volatile
   because the handler runs in an asynchronous context. */
static volatile sig_atomic_t usr1_hits;
static volatile sig_atomic_t usr1_signo;
static volatile sig_atomic_t usr1_code;
static volatile pid_t usr1_sender;
static volatile unsigned long usr1_rip;

static void usr1_handler(int sig, siginfo_t *si, void *uc) {
    usr1_signo = sig;
    if (si) {
        usr1_code = si->si_code;
        usr1_sender = si->_sifields._kill.si_pid;
    }
    if (uc) {
        ucontext_t *ctx = (ucontext_t *)uc;
        usr1_rip = (unsigned long)ctx->uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_RIP];
    }
    usr1_hits++;
}

static void sleep_ms(long ms) {
    struct timespec ts;
    ts.tv_sec = 0;
    ts.tv_nsec = ms * 1000000L;
    nanosleep(&ts, 0);
}

int main(int argc, char **argv, char **envp) {
    struct sigaction sa;
    sigset_t block;
    sigset_t saved;
    sigset_t pending;
    pid_t pid;
    pid_t r;
    int status;
    int hits;
    int i;

    puts("signals: main entered");
    CHECK(argc == 2 && argv[1] != 0 && strcmp(argv[1], "from-posix") == 0, "argv");
    (void)envp;

    /* A handler installed with SA_SIGINFO receives the siginfo and the
       machine context of the interrupted code. */
    sigemptyset(&sa.sa_mask);
    sa.sa_handler = usr1_handler;
    sa.sa_flags = SA_SIGINFO;
    sa.sa_restorer = 0;
    CHECK(sigaction(SIGUSR1, &sa, 0) == 0, "sigaction");

    pid = fork();
    CHECK(pid >= 0, "fork");
    if (pid == 0) {
        kill(getppid(), SIGUSR1);
        _exit(0);
    }
    /* The queued signal interrupts the wait. The handler runs before the
       syscall returns to user space, so the checks below see it. */
    status = 0;
    r = waitpid(pid, &status, 0);
    if (r != pid) {
        status = 0;
        r = waitpid(pid, &status, 0);
    }
    CHECK(r == pid, "child reaped");
    CHECK(WIFEXITED(status) && WEXITSTATUS(status) == 0, "child exit status");
    if (usr1_hits == 0) sleep_ms(2);
    CHECK(usr1_hits == 1, "handler ran once");
    CHECK(usr1_signo == SIGUSR1, "handler signal number");
    CHECK(usr1_code == SI_USER, "siginfo code");
    CHECK(usr1_sender == pid, "siginfo sender");
    CHECK(usr1_rip != 0, "ucontext instruction pointer");

    /* A signal whose action is SIG_IGN is discarded. */
    hits = usr1_hits;
    sigemptyset(&sa.sa_mask);
    sa.sa_handler = (void (*)(int))SIG_IGN;
    sa.sa_flags = 0;
    sa.sa_restorer = 0;
    CHECK(sigaction(SIGUSR2, &sa, 0) == 0, "sigaction ignore");
    pid = fork();
    CHECK(pid >= 0, "fork ignore");
    if (pid == 0) {
        kill(getppid(), SIGUSR2);
        _exit(0);
    }
    status = 0;
    r = waitpid(pid, &status, 0);
    CHECK(r == pid, "ignored child reaped");
    CHECK(usr1_hits == hits, "ignored signal discarded");

    /* A blocked signal stays pending until the mask allows the delivery. */
    hits = usr1_hits;
    sigemptyset(&block);
    sigaddset(&block, SIGUSR1);
    CHECK(sigprocmask(SIG_BLOCK, &block, &saved) == 0, "sigprocmask block");
    pid = fork();
    CHECK(pid >= 0, "fork blocked");
    if (pid == 0) {
        kill(getppid(), SIGUSR1);
        _exit(0);
    }
    status = 0;
    r = waitpid(pid, &status, 0);
    CHECK(r == pid, "blocked child reaped");
    CHECK(usr1_hits == hits, "blocked signal not delivered");
    CHECK(sigpending(&pending) == 0, "sigpending");
    CHECK(sigismember(&pending, SIGUSR1) == 1, "blocked signal pending");
    CHECK(sigprocmask(SIG_SETMASK, &saved, 0) == 0, "sigprocmask restore");
    CHECK(usr1_hits == hits + 1, "queued signal delivered after unblock");

    /* The default action of SIGKILL terminates the child. */
    pid = fork();
    CHECK(pid >= 0, "fork killed");
    if (pid == 0) {
        raise(SIGKILL);
        _exit(0);
    }
    status = 0;
    r = waitpid(pid, &status, 0);
    CHECK(r == pid, "killed child reaped");
    CHECK(WIFSIGNALED(status), "child killed by a signal");
    CHECK(WTERMSIG(status) == SIGKILL, "child signal number");

    /* A stopped process continues when SIGCONT arrives. The parent keeps
       sending SIGCONT because the signal may race with the stop. */
    pid = fork();
    CHECK(pid >= 0, "fork stopped");
    if (pid == 0) {
        raise(SIGSTOP);
        _exit(3);
    }
    status = 0;
    r = 0;
    for (i = 0; i < 500; i++) {
        kill(pid, SIGCONT);
        sleep_ms(1);
        r = waitpid(pid, &status, WNOHANG);
        if (r == pid) break;
    }
    CHECK(r == pid, "continued child reaped");
    CHECK(WIFEXITED(status) && WEXITSTATUS(status) == 3, "stopped child continued");

    if (failures) {
        printf("signals: %d checks failed\n", failures);
        return 1;
    }
    puts("signals: handler, mask, ignore, default and stop/continue passed");
    return 0;
}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: tests/hello-signal.c

FILE: tools/acceptance-test.sh

#!/bin/sh
set -u
ROOT=$(CDPATH= cd -- "$(dirname -- "$0")/.." && pwd)
cd "$ROOT"
LOG=${LOG:-build/acceptance-serial.log}
TIMEOUT=${TIMEOUT:-30}
mkdir -p build
rm -f "$LOG"
if ! ./tools/build-uefi.sh; then
  printf 'acceptance: build failed\n' >&2
  exit 1
fi
set +e
timeout "$TIMEOUT" ./tools/run-qemu.sh >"$LOG" 2>&1
status=$?
set -e
cat "$LOG"
# isa-debug-exit maps guest value 0x10 to host status 33.
if [ "$status" -ne 33 ]; then
  printf 'acceptance: unexpected QEMU status %s (expected 33)\n' "$status" >&2
  exit 1
fi
for text in \
  'local APIC timer and EOI passed' \
  'scheduler: context, FS base and zombie reap self-test passed' \
  'vfs: path lookup and file read self-test passed' \
  'execve: committed /bin/hello' \
  'argv, envp, VFS, VM, time, random and EFAULT passed' \
  'execve: committed /bin/hello-c' \
  'c runtime: main entered' \
  'c runtime: passed' \
  'execve: committed /bin/hello-posix' \
  'posix: main entered' \
  'posix: fork, pipe, dup2 and wait4 passed' \
  'execve: committed /bin/hello-signal' \
  'signals: main entered' \
  'signals: handler, mask, ignore, default and stop/continue passed' \
  'task exited status=0' \
  'process address space reclaimed status=0 vector=0'; do
  if ! grep -F "$text" "$LOG" >/dev/null; then
    printf 'acceptance: missing serial marker: %s\n' "$text" >&2
    exit 1
  fi
done
if grep -F 'acceptance failed' "$LOG" >/dev/null || grep -F 'kernel exception' "$LOG" >/dev/null; then
  printf 'acceptance: failure marker found\n' >&2
  exit 1
fi
printf 'acceptance: PASS\n'
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: tools/acceptance-test.sh

FILE: tools/build-initramfs.py
SIZE: 2038 bytes
SHA-256: 447db838e50b2e87bd30dd97a82cd21304b592b55f1dc7011bdcb4262c095692

#!/usr/bin/env python3
from pathlib import Path
import struct
out=bytearray(b'NSAR0001')
entries=[('/init',Path('build/minimal-linux.elf').read_bytes(),0o100755),('/bin/hello',Path('build/hello-linux.elf').read_bytes(),0o100755),('/bin/hello-c',Path('build/hello-c.elf').read_bytes(),0o100755),('/bin/hello-posix',Path('build/hello-posix.elf').read_bytes(),0o100755),('/bin/hello-signal',Path('build/hello-signal.elf').read_bytes(),0o100755),('/etc/motd',b'Slop/MNU initramfs\n',0o100444)]
out += struct.pack('<I',len(entries))
for name,data,mode in entries:
    b=name.encode(); out += struct.pack('<HHI',len(b),mode,len(data)); out += b; out += bytes((-len(out))&7); out += data; out += bytes((-len(out))&7)
Path('build/initramfs.nsar').write_bytes(out)

def coff_rdata(data,symbols):
    # Minimal COFF-x86-64 relocatable object with one .rdata section.
    dataoff=64
    symoff=(dataoff+len(data)+3)&~3
    strtab=bytearray(struct.pack('<I',0))
    names=[]
    for name,_ in symbols:
        b=name.encode()
        if len(b)<=8:
            names.append((b.ljust(8,b'\0'),None))
        else:
            names.append((b'\0\0\0\0'+struct.pack('<I',len(strtab)),len(strtab)))
            strtab+=b+b'\0'
    struct.pack_into('<I',strtab,0,len(strtab))
    header=struct.pack('<HHIIIHH',0x8664,1,0,symoff,len(symbols),0,0)
    section=struct.pack('<8sIIIIIIHHI',b'.rdata',0,0,len(data),dataoff,0,0,0,0,0x40500040)
    obj=bytearray(header+section)
    obj+=bytes(dataoff-len(obj))+data
    obj+=bytes(symoff-len(obj))
    for (nm,_),(_,value) in zip(names,symbols):
        obj+=struct.pack('<8sIHHBB',nm,value,1,0,2,0)
    obj+=strtab
    return bytes(obj)

blob=bytes(out)
syms=[('_binary_build_initramfs_nsar_start',0),('_binary_build_initramfs_nsar_end',len(blob)),('_binary_build_initramfs_nsar_size',len(blob))]
Path('build/initramfs.obj').write_bytes(coff_rdata(blob,syms))
print(f'created build/initramfs.nsar with {len(entries)} entries, {len(out)} bytes')
print(f'created build/initramfs.obj, {len(blob)} bytes of .rdata')
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: tools/build-initramfs.py

#!/bin/sh

  clang $CFLAGS -c "$src" -o "build/libc/$(basename "$src" .c).o"

  clang $CFLAGS -c "$src" -o "build/libc/$(basename "$src" .c).o"

LIBC="build/libc/start.o build/libc/syscall.o build/libc/sigreturn.o build/libc/string.o build/libc/stdio.o build/libc/stdlib.o build/libc/unistd.o build/libc/errno.o build/libc/signal.o"

#!/bin/sh
set -eu
mkdir -p build/efi/EFI/BOOT
./tools/check-toolchain.sh
./tools/build-test-elf.sh
./tools/build-libc-test.sh
./tools/build-initramfs.py
cargo +nightly build -Zbuild-std=core,compiler_builtins -Zbuild-std-features=compiler-builtins-mem --target x86_64-pc-windows-msvc --release
KLIB=$(find target/x86_64-pc-windows-msvc/release -maxdepth 1 -type f \( -name '*slop_mnu_kernel*.lib' -o -name '*slop_mnu_kernel*.a' \) | head -n 1)
test -n "$KLIB"
clang -target x86_64-pc-win32-coff -ffreestanding -fshort-wchar -mno-red-zone -fno-stack-protector -c loader/main.c -o build/loader.obj
for src in loader/entry.S arch/x86_64/exceptions.S arch/x86_64/syscall.S arch/x86_64/user.S arch/x86_64/irq.S arch/x86_64/context.S; do
  obj=build/$(basename "$src" .S)-coff.obj
  clang -target x86_64-pc-win32-coff -ffreestanding -mno-red-zone -c "$src" -o "$obj"
done
lld-link /subsystem:efi_application /entry:efi_main /nodefaultlib /machine:x64 \
  /out:build/efi/EFI/BOOT/BOOTX64.EFI \
  build/loader.obj build/entry-coff.obj build/exceptions-coff.obj build/syscall-coff.obj build/user-coff.obj build/irq-coff.obj build/context-coff.obj \
  build/initramfs.obj "$KLIB"
printf 'created build/efi/EFI/BOOT/BOOTX64.EFI\n'
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: tools/build-uefi.sh

FILE: tools/check-abi.py

SHA-256: 5d7e8fe6d459e76bdcd1b26a3150eeb12d493327351226224cdc22176c4e2cc3

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Check the Linux ABI layouts that the kernel publishes.

The script reads the kernel structures and the libc header and compares the
computed offsets with the published Linux x86-64 layouts. It needs no
compiler, so a layout drift is caught before a build.

Provenance for every expected value is recorded in docs/compat-ledger.md.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent

SIZES = {
    'u8': 1, 'i8': 1, 'u16': 2, 'i16': 2, 'u32': 4, 'i32': 4, 'u64': 8, 'i64': 8,
    'usize': 8, 'isize': 8, 'bool': 1,
}


class Types:
    """Resolves the primitive and the named structure sizes."""

    def __init__(self, structs):
        self.structs = structs
        self.sizes = {}
        self.aligns = {}

    def size_of(self, rust_type):
        rust_type = rust_type.strip()
        if rust_type in self.sizes:
            return self.sizes[rust_type]
        array = re.fullmatch(r'\[([^\];]+);\s*(\d+)\]', rust_type)
        if array:
            value = self.size_of(array.group(1)) * int(array.group(2))
            self.sizes[rust_type] = value
            self.aligns[rust_type] = self.align_of(array.group(1))
            return value
        if rust_type in SIZES:
            return SIZES[rust_type]
        if rust_type in self.structs:
            size, align = self.layout(rust_type)
            return size
        raise SystemExit('unknown type: %s' % rust_type)

    def align_of(self, rust_type):
        rust_type = rust_type.strip()
        if rust_type in self.aligns:
            return self.aligns[rust_type]
        array = re.fullmatch(r'\[([^\];]+);\s*(\d+)\]', rust_type)
        if array:
            return self.align_of(array.group(1))
        if rust_type in SIZES:
            return min(SIZES[rust_type], 8)
        if rust_type in self.structs:
            return self.layout(rust_type)[1]
        raise SystemExit('unknown type: %s' % rust_type)

    def layout(self, name):
        offset = 0
        offsets = {}
        for field, rust_type in self.structs[name]:
            align = self.align_of(rust_type)
            size = self.size_of(rust_type)
            offset = (offset + align - 1) & ~(align - 1)
            offsets[field] = offset
            offset += size
        size = (offset + 7) & ~7
        self.sizes[name] = size
        self.aligns[name] = min(self.align_of_of_field(name), 8)
        return offsets, size

    def align_of_of_field(self, name):
        align = 1
        for _, rust_type in self.structs[name]:
            align = max(align, self.align_of(rust_type))
        return align


def rust_structs(text):
    """Collects the plain field lists of the repr(C) structures."""
    found = {}
    for match in re.finditer(r'pub struct (\w+)\s*\{([^}]*)\}', text):
        name = match.group(1)
        fields = []
        for raw in match.group(2).split(','):
            raw = raw.strip()
            if not raw or raw.startswith('/*') or raw.startswith('#'):
                continue
            field = re.fullmatch(r'(?:pub )?(\w+)\s*:\s*([\w\[\]; ]+)', raw)
            if not field:
                continue
            fields.append((field.group(1), field.group(2).strip()))
        found[name] = fields
    return found


def main():
    kernel = (ROOT / 'kernel' / 'signal.rs').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    header = (ROOT / 'libc' / 'include' / 'signal.h').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    types = Types(rust_structs(kernel))
    failed = 0

    def expect(label, actual, expected):
        nonlocal failed
        if actual != expected:
            print('ABI MISMATCH %s: got %s, expected %s' % (label, actual, expected))
            failed += 1

    # struct k_sigaction: handler, flags, mask, restorer.
    offsets, size = types.layout('Action')
    expect('k_sigaction.handler', offsets['handler'], 0)
    expect('k_sigaction.flags', offsets['flags'], 8)
    expect('k_sigaction.mask', offsets['mask'], 16)
    expect('k_sigaction.restorer', offsets['restorer'], 24)
    expect('k_sigaction.size', size, 32)

    # struct sigcontext: the register order named by the REG_* index list.
    expected_context = {
        'r8': 0, 'r9': 8, 'r10': 16, 'r11': 24, 'r12': 32, 'r13': 40,
        'r14': 48, 'r15': 56, 'rdi': 64, 'rsi': 72, 'rbp': 80, 'rbx': 88,
        'rdx': 96, 'rax': 104, 'rcx': 112, 'rsp': 120, 'rip': 128,
        'eflags': 136, 'cs': 144, 'gs': 146, 'fs': 148, 'ss': 150,
        'err': 152, 'trapno': 160, 'oldmask': 168, 'cr2': 176,
        'fpstate': 184, 'reserved1': 192,
    }
    offsets, size = types.layout('SigContext')
    for name, expected in expected_context.items():
        expect('sigcontext.%s' % name, offsets[name], expected)
    expect('sigcontext.size', size, 256)

    # stack_t and the userspace ucontext_t.
    offsets, size = types.layout('StackArea')
    expect('stack_t.ss_sp', offsets['ss_sp'], 0)
    expect('stack_t.ss_flags', offsets['ss_flags'], 8)
    expect('stack_t.ss_size', offsets['ss_size'], 16)
    expect('stack_t.size', size, 24)

    offsets, size = types.layout('UContext')
    expect('ucontext.uc_flags', offsets['uc_flags'], 0)
    expect('ucontext.uc_link', offsets['uc_link'], 8)
    expect('ucontext.uc_stack', offsets['uc_stack'], 16)
    expect('ucontext.uc_mcontext', offsets['uc_mcontext'], 40)
    expect('ucontext.uc_sigmask', offsets['uc_sigmask'], 296)
    expect('ucontext.uc_fpstate', offsets['uc_fpstate'], 304)
    expect('ucontext.size', size, 816)

    # siginfo_t: the header fields and the _kill member inside the union.
    # The union starts right after the three header fields, so the _kill
    # member begins at offset 12.
    offsets, size = types.layout('SigInfo')
    expect('siginfo.si_signo', offsets['si_signo'], 0)
    expect('siginfo.si_errno', offsets['si_errno'], 4)
    expect('siginfo.si_code', offsets['si_code'], 8)
    expect('siginfo.si_pid', offsets['si_pid'], 12)
    expect('siginfo.si_uid', offsets['si_uid'], 16)
    expect('siginfo.size', size, 128)

    # The signal frame: the restorer, then the ucontext, then the siginfo.
    expect('frame.ucontext', 8, 8)
    expect('frame.siginfo', 8 + types.size_of('UContext'), 8 + 816)

    # The published REG_* index list has to match the register order above.
    registers = ['R8', 'R9', 'R10', 'R11', 'R12', 'R13', 'R14', 'R15',
                 'RDI', 'RSI', 'RBP', 'RBX', 'RDX', 'RAX', 'RCX', 'RSP',
                 'RIP', 'EFL', 'CSGSFS', 'ERR', 'TRAPNO', 'OLDMASK', 'CR2']
    for index, name in enumerate(registers):
        if ('#define REG_%s %d' % (name, index)) not in header:
            print('ABI MISMATCH libc signal.h: REG_%s is missing or has another index' % name)
            failed += 1

    # The libc header has to present the same layouts to the programs.
    ordered = [
        ('ucontext_t', ['uc_flags', 'uc_link', 'uc_stack', 'uc_mcontext',
                        'uc_sigmask', '__fpregs_mem']),
        ('siginfo_t', ['si_signo', 'si_errno', 'si_code', '_sifields']),
        ('stack_t', ['ss_sp', 'ss_flags', 'ss_size']),
    ]
    for name, fields in ordered:
        position = -1
        for field in fields:
            found = header.find(field, position + 1)
            if found < 0:
                print('ABI MISMATCH libc signal.h: %s misses %s' % (name, field))
                failed += 1
                continue
            position = found

    if failed:
        print('ABI layout checks FAILED (%d problems)' % failed)
        return 1
    print('ABI layout checks passed')
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: tools/check-abi.py

# Local mirror of tools/check-source.sh for environments without a POSIX shell.
# It parses the embedded Python table and verifies every required token.
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$root = Split-Path -Parent (Split-Path -Parent $MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path)
Set-Location $root
$script = Get-Content 'tools/check-source.sh' -Raw
$lines = $script -split "`n"
$required = @{}
foreach ($line in $lines) {
    if ($line -match "^\s*'([^']+)':\s*\[(.*)\],\s*$") {
        $file = $Matches[1]
        $tokens = $Matches[2] -split "," | ForEach-Object { $_.Trim().Trim("'") } | Where-Object { $_ -ne "" }
        $required[$file] = $tokens
    }
}
$failed = 0
foreach ($file in $required.Keys) {
    if (-not (Test-Path $file)) { Write-Output "MISSING FILE: $file"; $failed++; continue }
    $text = Get-Content $file -Raw
    foreach ($token in $required[$file]) {
        if (-not $text.Contains($token)) { Write-Output "MISSING TOKEN: $file -> $token"; $failed++ }
    }
}
$cargo = Get-Content 'Cargo.toml' -Raw
if (($cargo | Select-String -Pattern 'version = "0.29.0"' -AllMatches).Matches.Count -ne 1) {
    Write-Output "Cargo.toml version marker mismatch"; $failed++
}
if ($failed -gt 0) { Write-Output "source consistency checks FAILED ($failed)"; exit 1 }
Write-Output "source consistency checks passed"
$python = Get-Command python3 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
if (-not $python) { $python = Get-Command python -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
if ($python) {
    & $python.Source 'tools/check-abi.py'
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit 1 }
} else {
    Write-Output "no python interpreter found: skipping the ABI layout gate"
}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: tools/check-source.ps1

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Regenerate SOURCE_SNAPSHOT.txt from the working tree.

The snapshot records the size and the SHA-256 digest of every source file and
embeds the file contents, so that a release can be reconstructed from a single
archive. The layout is the one the earlier snapshots use, so the file can be
compared with them directly.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
OUTPUT = ROOT / 'SOURCE_SNAPSHOT.txt'
SKIP_DIRS = {'.git', 'build', 'target'}
SKIP_FILES = {'SOURCE_SNAPSHOT.txt'}
SEPARATOR = '=' * 80
RULE = '-' * 80


def version() -> str:
    for line in (ROOT / 'Cargo.toml').read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines():
        if line.startswith('version = '):
            return line.split('"')[1]
    raise SystemExit('Cargo.toml carries no version field')


def collect() -> list:
    files = []
    for path in ROOT.rglob('*'):
        if not path.is_file():
            continue
        rel = path.relative_to(ROOT)
        if rel.parts[0] in SKIP_DIRS:
            continue
        if rel.as_posix() in SKIP_FILES:
            continue
        files.append(rel.as_posix())
    return sorted(files)


def read_source(rel: str) -> bytes:
    """Reads a source file with the line endings of the repository.

    The repository stores the sources with Unix line endings, so the snapshot
    is taken over the normalized content. That keeps the digests independent
    of the line ending convention of the machine that runs the tool.
    """
    text = (ROOT / rel).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    return text.replace('\r\n', '\n').encode('utf-8')


def main() -> None:
    files = collect()
    lines = []
    lines.append('SLOP/MNU - COMPLETE SOURCE SNAPSHOT')
    lines.append('Version: %s' % version())
    lines.append('SLOP: Synthetic Low-quality Operating Platform')
    lines.append('MNU: Maybe Not Unix')
    lines.append('Encoding: UTF-8')
    lines.append('')
    lines.append('FILE MANIFEST')
    for rel in files:
        raw = read_source(rel)
        lines.append('%s%10d  %s' % (hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw), rel))
    lines.append('')
    lines.append('TOTAL FILES: %d' % len(files))
    lines.append('')
    for rel in files:
        raw = read_source(rel)
        text = raw.decode('utf-8')
        if text.endswith('\n'):
            text = text[:-1]
        lines.append(SEPARATOR)
        lines.append('FILE: %s' % rel)
        lines.append('SIZE: %d bytes' % len(raw))
        lines.append('SHA-256: %s' % hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest())
        lines.append(SEPARATOR)
        lines.append(text)
        lines.append(RULE)
        lines.append('END FILE: %s' % rel)
        lines.append('')
    with OUTPUT.open('w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n') as handle:
        handle.write('\n'.join(lines) + '\n')
    print('created %s with %d files' % (OUTPUT.name, len(files)))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: tools/make-snapshot.py

  -drive if=pflash,format=raw,readonly=on,file="$OVMF_CODE" \
  -drive if=pflash,format=raw,file=build/OVMF_VARS.fd \
  -drive format=raw,file=fat:rw:build/efi \
  -device isa-debug-exit,iobase=0xf4,iosize=0x04 \
  -serial stdio -display none -no-reboot -no-shutdown
